how much of the 5.2-5.4 ms Prefill inter-iteration gap remains after
prefill-async-admission (target below 1 ms, with no scheduler-thread
cudaStreamSynchronize over 1 ms), and the current Decode breakdown of
MegaMoE, FA3 verify, dense GEMM and small kernels.

line whose first-parent history contains cbca2b3. The tree equals main
8c18eab. Main's own first-parent history does not reach cbca2b3, so a
reuse run from main would select an older image; with this parent order
scripts/select-qwen-container.sh resolves build 40197369083, which matches
configs/firmus-framework.json (SHA-256 1f2476...5c55, 12272513024 bytes).
No build, kernel, overlay or serving change.

so the normal 60/120/30 pass repeats the 9120.51 and standalone 9132.03
conditions. The 20/30/90 profile client runs afterwards and does not
replace the normal score. Expected job time is about 16.5 minutes: the
same structure took 16:37 in 40071892077, and the normal pass now takes
about 13:38 without the removed QK-norm selection. That leaves roughly
20-40 seconds below the 17-minute limit; the normal score is written
before the profile pass starts.
